Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any projective module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 555. The projective resolution has length at most 121 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the projective space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 555 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 121. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the projective framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 948. The resulting operator achieves bound O(368/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by projective generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 948, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 368. QED.
